R09, SPM TM-E-1, s.8.1.4, PDF p32: fraud monitoring processes \"may involve suspending the transactions in a timely manner and/or contacting the customers concerned through a reliable channel to verify the transactions or activities\". Six hours with no offline alarms is not 72 hours with no indicator across the bank.\n   - Decided by: SEC, with the chair informed.\n8. Corporate email and the filing terminal.\n   - Default: treated as not safe until SEC logs them as safe (PH01 5.6, 5.7).\n   - Decided by: SEC.\n9. Technical suspension of log rotation and backup recycling.\n   - Release when: LC lifts the legal hold (PH01 17.1).\n   - Decided by: LC.\n\nHANDOVER (PH01 18.1–18.2)\nThe CMT hands over only items that have a named receiving owner who has signed acceptance of a specific list. Owners are given by role; names come from the CMT roster.\n- Standing security operations manager: N6 monitoring and detection rules; the quarantined host; perimeter blocks; hourly status reports to SEC until containment is confirmed, then every four hours; the N8 reduction review.\n- Head of IT Operations: gateway isolation infrastructure; the recovery build; the clock-synchronisation fix; changes to the privileged-access system.\n- Identity engineering lead: re-run and log the closure test on every path (overdue, due by 12:00 today); token and session lifecycle fixes.\n- Evidence custodian (an SEC designate): the evidence register, all holds and transfers, and the hashes of the partner deliveries.\n- Head of Payments Ops: the alternate-process state record (due by 10:00 today); the instruction register; items held as unknown status, including the two first-test mismatches if they map to real instructions; hourly reconciliation.\n- Forensic firm engagement owner (within SEC): indicator classification (N5, result outstanding); timeline versions; reconciling the vendor-service records.\n- Vendor relationship owner, with the Chief Risk Officer and LC: the vendor preservation request and its production (sending not verified); contract terms.\n- DPO, with LC: data-related items. This includes testing whether the 31 scam reports use data from the 320 file.\n- SEC (Head of Information Security): keeps all PH01 s.3 decision rights and signs any closure or release. Handover transfers the execution, not the authority (judgment).\n\nPAGE 1 OF THE HANDOVER PACK: \"NOT EVIDENCED\" (PH01 18.2). Each item transfers as OPEN, not done.\n- C01: validation results (A6); whether mobile submission was disabled under A5-bis; the 08:30 terminal and email logs; the content of the standby snapshot.\n- C02: whether the vendor preservation request was sent, and anything the vendor produced.\n- C03: closure-test result; access-window end; the 15:00 and 20:00 scope results; secret rotation; persistence sweep.\n- C04 and C06: SEC's clearance or hold of the alternate process; any releases and their reconciliation; the 20:15 status.\n- C05: suitability of the backup (I1–I4) and checks I5–I8 / S1–S7.\n- C06: the test-data preconditions for the first test (H2); the H3 flags; the content of the root cause.\n- C07: N2–N5 results; the independent review of the timeline.\n- C08: whether the 240-instruction test ran on the candidate (1) build, and whether its data preconditions were logged.\n\nOWNER\n- Accountable: SEC.\n- Receiving owners as listed above.\n- LC takes the register of external deadlines (PH01 18.4).\n\nTIMES\n- Handover pack signed before the CMT stands down.\n- Alternate-process record by 10:00 today.\n- Closure test and N2–N5 results by 12:00 today.\n- Full-set rerun after the root cause is documented.\n\nDESTINATION / CHANNEL\n- The handover pack, with signed acceptances.\n- Decision log, evidence register, instruction register.\n- The CMT reconvenes at once on any security trigger, missed external deadline or new data exposure (PH01 18.5).\n\nBASIS: EXTERNAL\n1. R08, SPM TM-G-1, s.4.2.4, PDF p18: \"only properly tested and approved systems are promoted to the production environment\". A sample is not a full test, so the production hold stays.\n2. R03, HKMA Sectoral Code for Designated AIs, s.6.2.26(a)–(b), PDF p19: detect incidents affecting the critical computer systems, monitor threat intelligence, \"and take appropriate actions\". This keeps monitoring raised while the indicator is unclassified.\n3. R06, SPM OR-2, s.8.3–8.4, PDF p13. The incident management programme covers the \"full life-cycle of any incidents\" and is \"supported by an inventory of internal and third party resources\". This supports a role-by-role handover.\n4. R02, OCCICS Code of Practice v1.0, s.7.2.3(c), PDF p33: ensure team members \"are familiar with both their own roles and responsibilities and those of other team members\". This supports named owners with signed acceptance.\n5. R07, SPM TM-G-2, s.4.3.2, PDF p11: the full recovery phase \"is as difficult and critical to the business as the process to activate the BCP\".\n\nNO DIRECT RULE LOCATED externally for handover mechanics.\n- Term \"handover\": hits only PH01.\n- Other terms searched: \"stand-down\", \"monitor\".\n\nBASIS: INTERNAL (Internal bank procedure: PH01, v1.0, effective 1 Oct 2026)\n- s.18.1: \"The CMT hands over to standing teams only when each open item has a named receiving owner who has signed acceptance of a specific list.\"\n- s.18.2: \"Each handover pack starts with a page listing what is not evidenced. Every change to a critical computer system is logged with its deployment date for LC under Cap. 653 s.22 (PH01 18.4).\n\n- RC-A, vendor access model. Owner: Head of IT Ops, with SEC.\n  - Enforce PH01 7.2: individual named accounts, multi-factor authentication, time-limited sessions through the privileged-access system, recorded sessions. No shared accounts.\n  - Whether the compromised account was shared is still unverified.\n- RC-B, session and token lifecycle. Owner: identity engineering lead.\n  - Revocation must reach every path: identity-provider sessions, refresh tokens, server-side application sessions, maximum session lifetimes. C03 showed that a revoked credential could keep live sessions.\n- RC-C, segmentation and egress. Owner: Head of IT Ops.\n  - The vendor path lands only on jump hosts, with no route to customer-data stores.\n  - Outbound controls and data-loss-prevention alerts on the workspace.\n- RC-D, data minimisation. Owner: the data owner, with the DPO and SEC.\n  - Remove or mask customer identity data in support workspaces.\n  - Access only where needed (R16b Annex C).\n- RC-E, detection. Owner: security operations manager.\n  - Alerts on vendor-account behaviour, bulk exports and out-of-window logons.\n- RC-F, recovery reconciliation. Owners: IT Ops recovery lead and Head of Payments Ops.\n  - Document the cause of the 118/120 failure.\n  - Run a full-set RC1–RC8 rerun.\n- RC-G, supplier contract terms. Owners: vendor relationship owner, the Chief Risk Officer and LC.\n  - Log retention, audit and production rights, incident cooperation, security requirements.\n- RC-H, clock synchronisation. Owner: Head of IT Ops.\n  - All critical-system components synchronised to the primary time source (PH01 6.4). Clocks differed by up to four minutes on this incident.\n- RC-I, secrets. Owner: identity engineering lead.\n  - Rotate everything the old access could read (PH01 7.5).\n  - Where a supplier uses a password as the only factor, change it after every use.\n\nDue dates (judgment):\n- Remediation plan with owners and dates by 26 Nov.\n- Technical root-cause draft to LC by 25 Nov. It feeds the Annex F written report, which LC files about 30 Nov (14 days after awareness on 16 Nov).\n- Post-incident review completed by 16 Dec.\n\nEVIDENCE TO CLOSE THE VENDOR-CREDENTIAL ISSUE\nSEC signs the closure in writing. Each item is logged with its evidence ID. Closing the issue is NOT reinstatement.\n- V1, inventory: a complete list of the vendor's accounts, keys, certificates, tokens and API credentials on every system: identity provider, VPN, privileged access, gateway, workspace, directory, alternate process and recovery environment. It must be reconciled with the vendor's own account records (PH01 6.7(d)), with no unreconciled entry left.\n- V2: disablement, revocation and key-removal records, with timestamps, for every item in V1.\n- V3: closure test passed on EVERY path. (iv) Improvement actions recommended.\"\n3. ## Lead's instructions for this scoring (verbatim, Chinese)
按实质判分。引文能定位、检索能用日志复核，就照证据给分。触发条件、时限、收件方或行动门槛用错，仍按原口径判。主持人记为「无法核实」的事项，按角色当时看到的交接判断。汇总里只列真正可能改分、需要组员拍板的题。

## Rubric and expectations (read these first)
- Scoring rules: `岚汐银行-72小时勒索软件桌面推演.txt` line 20 (how to record CEO CONFIRM/OVERRIDE as C/O) and section 五 (lines 249–300: codes OK/ERR/MISS/GAP/NC/U, 行动分, 依据分).
- Regulatory layer expectations (sealed v2): `组员预注册/岚汐银行-A版监管侧预期-v2封存.md` — read "监管预期和评分判据" (lines 55–61) and your units' entries (Y/N/U, 原文定位, 可接受行动, 决策码, 检索与核验).
- Manual layer expectations (sealed v1): `组员预注册/岚汐银行-B版手册侧预期-v1封存.md` — read "判法" (lines 9–22, including the four cautions) and your units' entries (Y / Y（混合） / N, 依据, 适用条件).
- The sealed score sheet `组员预注册/岚汐银行-B1评分表-v1封存.csv` is reference only.
- PH01 text: `推演记录/岚汐运行包（B版）/sources/text/PH01 Lan Tidal Bank Cyber Incident Response Playbook.txt`. All frozen sources are in that `text/` folder (page markers `=== PDF page N ===`).

## Codes and how to apply them
- **R判定** (regulatory layer, by v2's Y/N/U and branch): OK = location, wording, normative status and applicability right and enough for the specific decision; ERR = a citation error that affects the decision (invented text, wrong time limit, wrong recipient, wrong scope, internal procedure presented as a regulatory requirement); MISS = v2 says a direct rule exists but the answer did not find/use it or cited only broad principles; GAP = v2 says N, the role gave a checkable search record and marked the rest as professional judgment; NC = v2 says N but no adequate search record or judgment label; U = facts or sources insufficient (v2 U).
- **PH判定** (manual layer, by the manual-side Y / Y（混合） / N): OK = the needed PH01 clause(s) cited and applied with the right owner, threshold, step or timing; for Y（混合） judge only the part PH01 covers (the external triggers, time limits and recipients stay in the regulatory layer); MISS = PH01's direct rule for that part not used (or only a broad reference); ERR = PH01 misapplied in a way that affects the decision, or a statutory trigger/time limit/recipient attributed to PH01 (caution 1); GAP = manual N and the role's search record covers PH01 with the rest marked judgment; NC = manual N without adequate record. PH01 internal numbers presented as regulatory requirements are judged in the regulatory layer (caution 2).
- **行动分** 0/1: executable on the card facts; action, owner, time or trigger, recipient or destination complete; meets the preset necessary conditions (v2's 可接受行动 and the manual-side 适用条件). A hold earns the point if it says who reviews, when or on what condition it restarts, and to whom it passes.
- **依据分**: 1 if R and PH are both OK or GAP; 0 if either is ERR, MISS or NC; otherwise (U present, no 0-code) the string "待复核" (not counted in the denominator).
- **CEO layer C/O**: C if the CEO selects a branch stated in the original answer and keeps its action, timing, recipient and conditions; O if the CEO's decision (including a conditional approval) changes action, timing, recipient or threshold; adding a reason or filling a pure record field stays C. Also record the CEO's own label (CONFIRM/OVERRIDE) as written and note any discrepancy. Score the CEO's final decision on its own merits (its basis and action). The two layers normally share R判定 unless the CEO corrected or introduced a citation error — say so if they differ.
- Use the lead's finalized A1 rulings as precedents where B1 has the same issue: `推演记录/A1/核验/A1 评分底稿汇总.md` (sections 四–六: e.g. Cap. 455 s.25A(2) is not a general payment precondition; a wallet-address test is the wrong s.25A(1) trigger; R09 §4.3.2 needs the "false impression" condition; R09 §8.2.3 "shortly after the commencement" is for the first announcement only; Sch.6 item 1 splits 12 h / 48 h by effect on the core function; test data needs de-sensitisation and prior approval). A1's per-card drafts show the format and how v2 was applied: `推演记录/A1/核验/评分底稿_C01-C02.md` and `.jsonl`; A1's final per-unit codes after all rounds are in `推演记录/A1/核验/A1评分底稿_明细.csv`. A1 records: `推演记录/A1/C01/`, `C02/`.

## B1 evidence
- Sealed answers: `推演记录/B1/C01/` and `C02/`: `SEC.raw.md`, `LC.raw.md`, `COM.raw.md`, `CEO.final.md`. Exactly what each role received (including facilitator handoffs): `input.<ROLE>.txt`. Facilitator status registration (Chinese): `handoff.md`. Run record `推演记录/B1/00 运行记录.md` section 六 explains registration choices (e.g. C01 Q03b recorded "cannot be verified" because COM's own default without LC clearance was to hold).
- Search and read logs: `推演记录/读取记录/B1/<ROLE>.source_calls.jsonl` (each call: tool, args, ok, hits per doc, pages). Use them to verify search records and "no direct rule located" claims (terms, documents searched) and that cited pages were read.
- Quote check output: `推演记录/B1/核验/citations.csv` (status OK / OK-NOPAGE / PAGE / DOC / FRAGMENTS / NOT-FOUND per quoted passage). The facilitator's triage so far: NOT-FOUND entries are mostly role-drafted wording, search terms, or genuine quotes split by a running header across a page break; DOC/PAGE are mostly attribution or page-parse artifacts (e.g. "Cap. 653" read as a page). No fabricated quote found so far. Check any quote that a unit's basis depends on yourself against the text.

## Output format
JSONL, one object per unit per layer (24 lines: Q01a, Q01b, …, Q06b; for each "原答" then "CEO最终"), keys exactly:
`unit`, `layer` ("原答" or "CEO最终"), `R判定`, `PH判定`, `行动分` (0 or 1), `依据分` (0, 1 or "待复核"), `决策码` (v2's normalized decision code plus key scope, time and object in this run), `CO` ("" for 原答; C/O/U for CEO), `CEO自标` ("" for 原答; CONFIRM/OVERRIDE as written), `记录索引` (file and line ranges, e.g. `推演记录/B1/C01/SEC.raw.md 第11–49行`), `理由` (Chinese, specific: which v2 rule / PH01 section, which quotes and pages you checked, which search terms you confirmed in the logs, why each code), `需组员定` (true only if the call could realistically change a score and needs a group ruling), `问题` (the question for the group, else ""), `PH依据` (PH01 sections actually cited for this unit, e.g. "§7.1, §3, §6.1"), `A1对照` (A1's final codes for the same unit and layer, e.g. "A1: R GAP / PH NA / 行动1 / 依据1 / C"), `例子候选` ("" or one Chinese sentence: A1 handling → B1's PH01 clause → what the CEO adopted → concrete improvement).
The .md file (plain Chinese, like the A1 drafts): method notes, one table with both layers per unit, notes on any 需组员定 items, and the example candidates.

## What to return to me
A short Chinese summary: per unit and layer the codes (R/PH/行动/依据, plus C/O and CEO label for the CEO layer); totals for your two cards (行动分 sum, 依据分 sum/denominator, counts of R and PH codes, C/O counts and label discrepancies); the 需组员定 items with the question; up to 3 report-example candidates with the four steps (A1 → B1 PH01 clause → CEO adopted → improvement). Aim to finish within about 25 minutes; be precise rather than exhaustive in prose.

PH01 internal numbers presented as regulatory requirements are judged in the regulatory layer (caution 2).
- **行动分** 0/1: executable on the card facts; action, owner, time or trigger, recipient or destination complete; meets the preset necessary conditions (v2's 可接受行动 and the manual-side 适用条件). A hold earns the point if it says who reviews, when or on what condition it restarts, and to whom it passes.
- **依据分**: 1 if R and PH are both OK or GAP; 0 if either is ERR, MISS or NC; otherwise (U present, no 0-code) the string "待复核" (not counted in the denominator).
- **CEO layer C/O**: C if the CEO selects a branch stated in the original answer and keeps its action, timing, recipient and conditions; O if the CEO's decision (including a conditional approval) changes action, timing, recipient or threshold; adding a reason or filling a pure record field stays C. Also record the CEO's own label (CONFIRM/OVERRIDE) as written and note any discrepancy. Score the CEO's final decision on its own merits (its basis and action). The two layers normally share R判定 unless the CEO corrected or introduced a citation error — say so if they differ.
- Use the lead's finalized A1 rulings as precedents where B1 has the same issue: `推演记录/A1/核验/A1 评分底稿汇总.md` (sections 四–六: e.g. Cap. 455 s.25A(2) is not a general payment precondition; a wallet-address test is the wrong s.25A(1) trigger; R09 §4.3.2 needs the "false impression" condition; R09 §8.2.3 "shortly after the commencement" is for the first announcement only — A1's Q12a was ERR for using it as the basis of a later update; Sch.6 item 1 splits 12 h / 48 h by effect on the core function; test data needs de-sensitisation and prior approval). **总分（B1 两层分数完全一样，A1 用定稿成绩）**

| | 行动分 | 依据分 | 监管层 | 手册层 |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| A1 | 46/48 | 37/44，另 4 行待复核 | OK 20、GAP 17、ERR 7、U 4 | NA |

- 待复核的 4 题和 A1 是同样四题，所以分母都是 44。
- 逐题比，监管层只有 4 题和 A1 不同：
  - Q13b：行动分加 1；
  - Q23a：行动、依据各加 1；
  - Q24a：依据加 1；

- A1 的 4 个外部法律错读，B1 照样犯了：Q05a、Q14a 的第 25A 条披露条件，Q14b、Q15b 的 R09 第 4.3.2 段触发条件。
- CEO 层按规则记确认 30、推翻 18。CEO 自己标的确认 32、推翻 16，有 2 处出入（Q21a、Q22a），不影响分数。

**需要组员拍板的 3 个问题（只列会改分的）**
1. **Q06a 原答的手册层，判 OK 还是错引？** COM 先发了过渡指示，事后才请 LC 确认，而 PH01 §3 要求员工话术由 LC 审定。判错引的话，原答层依据分减 1。
2. **Q12a 监管层，判“检索记录不足”还是 GAP？** COM 这张卡没写外部检索记录，但在同一会话的前两张卡检索过。改 GAP 的话，两层依据分各加 1。
3. **Q24a、Q24b 监管层，维持 GAP 还是改“检索记录不足”？** COM 在 C08 没有调用检索工具，只列了前几轮读过的页。改的话，两层依据分各减 2，Q24a 比 A1 的进步也就没了。

第 2、3 题是同一类问题，宜一并定。三题怎么取舍，依据分在每层 37/44 到 40/44 之间。

另有七题，角色自述的检索词有一两个在日志里查不到。我按你给的“检索能用日志复核，就照证据给分”统一处理：日志里实际做过的检索已经足够支持结论，维持 GAP，不再列为待定。查不到的词都在各行理由里写明了。

**能写进报告的 4 个例子（都已对过原文）**
- **Q13b 交易对账：** A1 用真实账册测试，没写去敏和事先批准，行动 0 分。B1 按 PH01 §9.2 把两项写成前提，CEO 原样确认，行动得 1 分。同一条合格线到 C06 直接把 118/120 判为不合格。
- **Q23a 重大变更通报：** A1 把各项变更并成一个截止日，判错引，行动、依据都是 0。B1 按 PH01 §18.4 逐项按部署日计时，CEO 照采并加了一项（第 27(4) 条：应急计划修订后一个月内报专员）。两项都由 0 变 1。
- **Q24a 收尾公告：** A1 错用首次公告的条款，依据 0 分。B1 按 PH01 §11.5 的五项收尾条件逐项核对，CEO 采纳，只把一句没核实的默认措辞改掉。依据由 0 变 1，前提是第 3 题维持 GAP。
- **Q16a 恢复闸门：** A1 的 CEO 写了闸门条件可以交董事会豁免。B1 按 PH01 §3“No gate condition may be waived”，董事会只能收窄范围、不能免除条件，CEO 照采。分数不变，但裁决改善了。

PH01 也有管不到的地方：手册本身不复述外部规则的触发条件，所以外部法律层的错读它纠正不了，那 4 题 B1 的手册层仍判 OK。

英文报告已经写完，都在[工作文档](https://claude.ai/artifact/NQB9rpxw8tnuT8qZYiiURm)的“报告”页里，用的是已采信的 B1 成绩。

**写了什么**
- **第 1 部分**：执行摘要 298 词。
- **第 5 部分**：从 PH01 选了六项控制，每项写清谁负责、什么条件下执行、留下什么记录，并配一条 B1 的具体决定。六项是危机指挥、监管通报、客户通知、备用支付、恢复和交接。
